readByte() return b1 + b2 * 256 end local function readUint32() local b1 = readByte() local b2 = readByte() local b3 = readByte() local b4 = readByte() return b1 + b2 * 256 + b3 * 65536 + b4 * 16777216 end local function readInt16() local val = readUint16() if val >= 32768 then return val - 65536 end return val end local function parse() local proto_id = proto_counter proto_counter = proto_counter + 1 local code_key = derive(master, proto_id * 2 + 1, 65536) local const_key = derive(master, proto_id * 2 + 2, 256) local p = {} p[8] = readByte() local codeLength = readUint32() local raw_code = {} for i = 1, codeLength do raw_code[i] = readUint16() end local codeMac = readUint16() local code = setmetatable({}, { __index = function(_, k) local enc = raw_code[k] if not enc then return nil end local dec = bxor(enc, (code_key * 3 + k * 13) % 65536) if dec >= 32768 then return dec - 65536 end return dec end, __newindex = function() error(decode_string(1)) end, __metatable = decode_string(1) }) p[3] = code p[9] = codeMac p[10] = codeLength p[11] = raw_code local capturedLength = readUint16() local captured = {} for i = 1, capturedLength do captured[i] = readByte() end p[7] = captured local protosLength = readUint16() local protos = {} for i = 1, protosLength do protos[i] = parse() end p[5] = protos p[2] = readByte() == 1 p[1] = readByte() local constsLength = readUint16() local encoded_cells = {} for i = 1, constsLength do local t = readByte() if t == 1 then local len = readUint32() local salt = readUint32() local salt2 = readUint16() local mode = readByte() local check = readUint16() local raw = {} for j = 1, len do raw[j] = readByte() end encoded_cells[i] = { t = 1, raw = raw, len = len, salt = salt, salt2 = salt2, mode = mode, check = check } elseif t == 2 then local len = readUint16() local raw = {} for j = 1, len do raw[j] = readByte() end encoded_cells[i] = { t = 2, raw = raw, len = len } elseif t == 3 then encoded_cells[i] = { t = 3, bv = readByte() == 1 } elseif t == 4 then encoded_cells[i] = { t = 4 } end end local pow8 = { 1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64, 128, 256 } local powByte = { 1, 256, 65536, 16777216 } local powCheck = { 1, 256 } local key_fragments = { (const_key + 37) % 256, (const_key * 3 + 91) % 256, (const_key * 5 + 23) % 256 } local function key_piece_a() return (key_fragments[1] + 219) % 256 end local function key_piece_b() return ((key_fragments[2] + 165) * 171) % 256 end local function key_piece_c() return ((key_fragments[3] + 233) * 205) % 256 end local function rebuild_const_key(noise) local a = key_piece_a() local b = key_piece_b() local c = key_piece_c() if noise % 3 == 1 then return bxor(bxor(a, b), b) elseif noise % 3 == 2 then return bxor(bxor(c, a), c) end return a end local fake_key_box = { (const_key * 7 + 19) % 256, (const_key * 11 + 73) % 256, 151 } local function fake_decrypt_probe(cell, seed) local noise = (seed + fake_key_box[1] + fake_key_box[2]) % 256 if cell and cell.raw and cell.raw[1] then noise = bxor(noise, cell.raw[1]) end return (noise + fake_key_box[3]) % 256 end local function rotl8(v, r) r = r % 8 return ((v * pow8[r + 1]) % 256) + m_floor(v / pow8[8 - r + 1]) end local function rotr8(v, r) r = r % 8 return m_floor(v / pow8[r + 1]) + ((v % pow8[r + 1]) * pow8[8 - r + 1]) % 256 end local function update_string_check(chk, b, pos, salt2) return (chk + b * ((pos % 251) + 1) + ((chk * 31 + salt2 + pos * 7) % 65536)) % 65536 end local function open_string_cell(cell, const_index) local key = rebuild_const_key(cell.salt + const_index + cell.mode) local check_mask = bxor(derive(key + 1, (cell.salt % 65521) + const_index * 13 + cell.salt2, 65536), m_floor(cell.salt / 65536) % 65536) local expected_check = bxor(cell.check, check_mask) local state = (cell.salt + key * 65537 + (const_index + 1) * 1103515245 + cell.len * 131 + cell.salt2) % 4294967296 local prev = bxor(bxor(m_floor(cell.salt / 256) % 256, cell.salt2 % 256), key) local out = {} local actual_check = (cell.salt + cell.salt2 * 257 + key * 131 + (const_index + 1) * 17 + cell.len) % 65536 if (cell.salt + cell.len + const_index) % 5 == 2 then fake_decrypt_probe(cell, const_index) end for j = 1, cell.len do local enc = cell.raw[j] state = (state * 1664525 + 1013904223 + cell.salt2 + j * 97) % 4294967296 local key_byte = bxor(bxor(bxor(m_floor(state / 16777216) % 256, cell.salt2 % 256), (key * 17) % 256), (j * 29) % 256) local salt_byte = m_floor(cell.salt / powByte[((j - 1) % 4) + 1]) % 256 local mutate = (salt_byte + j * 13 + cell.salt2 + const_index * 11) % 256 local rot = (cell.salt2 + j + key + (state % 7)) % 8 local mask = (const_index * 29 + j * 17 + key + (cell.salt % 256)) % 256 local check_byte = m_floor(expected_check / powCheck[((j - 1) % 2) + 1]) % 256 local dec if cell.mode == 0 then local x4 = bxor(enc, mask) local x3 = (x4 - prev - check_byte) % 256 local x2 = bxor(x3, key_byte) local x1 = rotr8(x2, rot) dec = (x1 - mutate) % 256 elseif cell.mode == 1 then local x3 = bxor(enc, mask) local x2 = rotr8(x3, rot) local x1 = (x2 - mutate - prev) % 256 dec = bxor(x1, key_byte) else local x3 = (enc - prev - check_byte - mask) % 256 local x2 = bxor(x3, key_byte) local x1 = (x2 - mutate) % 256 dec = rotr8(x1, rot) end actual_check = update_string_check(actual_check, dec, j, cell.salt2) out[j] = s_char(dec) prev = enc end if actual_check ~= expected_check then error(decode_string(2), 0) end return t_concat(out) end local consts = setmetatable({}, { __index = function(tbl, k) local cell = encoded_cells[k] if not cell then return nil end local val local i = k - 1 if cell.t == 1 then val = open_string_cell(cell, i) elseif cell.t == 2 then local s = {} local state = (const_key * 16777216 + i * 1664525 + 1013904223) % 4294967296 for j = 1, cell.len do state = (state * 1664525 + 1013904223) % 4294967296 local key_byte = m_floor(state / 16777216) % 256 local dec = bxor(cell.raw[j], key_byte) s[j] = s_char(dec) end val = tonumber(t_concat(s)) elseif cell.t == 3 then val = cell.bv elseif cell.t == 4 then val = nil end rawset(tbl, k, val) return val end, __metatable = decode_string(1), __newindex = function() error(decode_string(1)) end, __pairs = function() error(decode_string(1), 0) end, __ipairs = function() error(decode_string(1), 0) end }) p[4] = consts p[6] = code_key local locked = { __metatable = decode_string(1), __newindex = function() error(decode_string(1)) end, __index = p, __pairs = function() error(decode_string(1), 0) end, __ipairs = function() error(decode_string(1), 0) end, __tostring = function() return decode_string(1) end } local proxy = setmetatable({}, locked) return proxy end return parse() end local b85_alphabet = decode_string(66) local b85_values = {} for i = 1, #b85_alphabet do b85_values[s_byte(b85_alphabet, i)] = i - 1 end local function decode_base85(str) str = s_gsub(str, decode_string(67), decode_string(68)) str = s_sub(str, 7) return s_gsub(str, decode_string(69), function(x) local b1, b2, b3, b4, b5 = s_byte(x, 1, 5) local a = b85_values[b5] + b85_values[b4] * 85 + b85_values[b3] * 7225 + b85_values[b2] * 614125 + b85_values[b1] * 52200625 local out1 = a % 256 a = m_floor(a / 256) local out2 = a % 256 a = m_floor(a / 256) local out3 = a % 256 a = m_floor(a / 256) local out4 = a % 256 return s_char(out1, out2, out3, out4) end) end local
